```
   145→func TestDiffLines_ModifiedEstimation(t *testing.T) {
   146→	// Test diffLines with modifications (additions + removals)
   147→	// When we have both additions and removals, min(added, removed) represents modifications
   148→	checkpoint := "original1\noriginal2\noriginal3\n"
   149→	committed := "modified1\nmodified2\noriginal3\nnew line\n"
   150→
   151→	unchanged, added, removed := diffLines(checkpoint, committed)
   152→
   153→	// original3 is unchanged (1)
   154→	// original1, original2 removed (2)
   155→	// modified1, modified2, new line added (3)
   156→	if unchanged != 1 {
   157→		t.Errorf("expected 1 unchanged, got %d", unchanged)
   158→	}
   159→	if added != 3 {
   160→		t.Errorf("expected 3 added, got %d", added)
   161→	}
   162→	if removed != 2 {
   163→		t.Errorf("expected 2 removed, got %d", removed)
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Estimate modified lines: min(3, 2) = 2 modified
   167→	// humanModified = 2
   168→	// humanAdded = 3 - 2 = 1 (pure additions)
   169→	// humanRemoved = 2 - 2 = 0 (pure removals)
   170→	humanModified := min(added, removed)
   171→	humanAdded := added - humanModified
   172→	humanRemoved := removed - humanModified
   173→
   174→	if humanModified != 2 {
   175→		t.Errorf("expected 2 modified, got %d", humanModified)
   176→	}
   177→	if humanAdded != 1 {
   178→		t.Errorf("expected 1 pure added (after subtracting modified), got %d", humanAdded)
   179→	}
   180→	if humanRemoved != 0 {
   181→		t.Errorf("expected 0 pure removed (after subtracting modified), got %d", humanRemoved)
   182→	}
   183→}
   184→
   185→// buildTestTree creates an object.Tree from a map of file paths to content.
   186→// This is a test helper for creating trees without a full git repository.
   187→func buildTestTree(t *testing.T, files map[string]string) *object.Tree {
   188→	t.Helper()
   189→
   190→	if len(files) == 0 {
   191→		return nil
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Use memory storage to build a tree
   195→	storage := memory.NewStorage()
   196→
   197→	// Create blob objects for each file
   198→	var entries []object.TreeEntry
   199→	for path, content := range files {
   200→		// Encode the blob
   201→		obj := storage.NewEncodedObject()
   202→		obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
   203→		writer, err := obj.Writer()
   204→		if err != nil {
   205→			t.Fatalf("failed to create blob writer: %v", err)
   206→		}
   207→		if _, err := writer.Write([]byte(content)); err != nil {
   208→			t.Fatalf("failed to write blob content: %v", err)
   209→		}
   210→		writer.Close()
   211→
   212→		// Store the blob
   213→		hash, err := storage.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   214→		if err != nil {
   215→			t.Fatalf("failed to store blob: %v", err)
   216→		}
   217→
   218→		// Create tree entry
   219→		entries = append(entries, object.TreeEntry{
   220→			Name: path,
   221→			Mode: 0o100644,
   222→			Hash: hash,
   223→		})
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Sort entries by name (required by git tree format)
   227→	sort.Slice(entries, func(i, j int) bool {
   228→		return entries[i].Name < entries[j].Name
   229→	})
   230→
   231→	// Create the tree
   232→	tree := &object.Tree{
   233→		Entries: entries,
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Encode and store the tree
   237→	obj := storage.NewEncodedObject()
   238→	obj.SetType(plumbing.TreeObject)
   239→	if err := tree.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   240→		t.Fatalf("failed to encode tree: %v", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	hash, err := storage.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		t.Fatalf("failed to store tree: %v", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	// Retrieve the tree
   249→	treeObj, err := object.GetTree(storage, hash)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		t.Fatalf("failed to get tree: %v", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	return treeObj
   255→}

   504→		t.Fatal("expected non-nil result")
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	// Expected:
   508→	// - Agent added 0 lines
   509→	// - User added 2 lines
   510→	// - Total = 0 + 2 = 2
   511→	// - Agent percentage = 0%
   512→
   513→	if result.AgentLines != 0 {
   514→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, want 0", result.AgentLines)
   515→	}
   516→	if result.HumanAdded != 2 {
   517→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 2", result.HumanAdded)
   518→	}
   519→	if result.HumanModified != 0 {
   520→		t.Errorf("HumanModified = %d, want 0", result.HumanModified)
   521→	}
   522→	if result.HumanRemoved != 0 {
   523→		t.Errorf("HumanRemoved = %d, want 0", result.HumanRemoved)
   524→	}
   525→	if result.TotalCommitted != 2 {
   526→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, want 2", result.TotalCommitted)
   527→	}
   528→	if result.AgentPercentage != 0.0 {
   529→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want 0.0%%", result.AgentPercentage)
   530→	}
   531→}

   533→// TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserRemovesAllAgentLines tests when
   534→// the user removes all lines the agent added.
   535→func TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserRemovesAllAgentLines(t *testing.T) {
   536→	// Base: empty file
   537→	baseTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   538→		"main.go": "",
   539→	})
   540→
   541→	// Shadow (agent work): agent adds 5 lines
   542→	shadowTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   543→		"main.go": "agent1\nagent2\nagent3\nagent4\nagent5\n",
   544→	})
   545→
   546→	// Head (final commit): user removed all agent lines and added their own
   547→	headTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   548→		"main.go": "user1\nuser2\nuser3\n",
   549→	})
   550→
   551→	filesTouched := []string{"main.go"}
   552→	promptAttributions := []PromptAttribution{} // No intermediate checkpoints
   553→
   554→	result := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   555→		baseTree, shadowTree, headTree, filesTouched, promptAttributions,
   556→	)
   557→
   558→	if result == nil {
   559→		t.Fatal("expected non-nil result")
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	// Expected:
   563→	// - Agent added 5 lines (base → shadow)
   564→	// - User added 3 lines, removed 5 lines (shadow → head)
   565→	// - humanModified = min(3, 5) = 3
   566→	// - pureUserAdded = 3 - 3 = 0
   567→	// - pureUserRemoved = 5 - 3 = 2
   568→	// - agentLinesInCommit = 5 - 2 - 3 = 0
   569→	// - Total = 5 + 0 - 2 = 3
   570→	// - Agent percentage = 0/3 = 0%
   571→
   572→	if result.AgentLines != 0 {
   573→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, want 0 (all agent lines removed/modified)", result.AgentLines)
   574→	}
   575→	if result.HumanAdded != 0 {
   576→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 0 (all counted as modifications)", result.HumanAdded)
   577→	}
   578→	if result.HumanModified != 3 {
   579→		t.Errorf("HumanModified = %d, want 3", result.HumanModified)
   580→	}
   581→	if result.HumanRemoved != 2 {
   582→		t.Errorf("HumanRemoved = %d, want 2", result.HumanRemoved)
   583→	}
   584→	if result.TotalCommitted != 3 {
   585→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, want 3", result.TotalCommitted)
   586→	}
   587→	if result.AgentPercentage != 0.0 {
   588→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want 0.0%%", result.AgentPercentage)
   589→	}
   590→}

   592→// TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_WithPromptAttributions tests with
   593→// accumulated user edits captured between checkpoints.
   594→func TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_WithPromptAttributions(t *testing.T) {
   595→	// Base: empty file
   596→	baseTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   597→		"main.go": "",
   598→	})
   599→
   600→	// Shadow (final checkpoint): includes agent work (10 lines) + user work between checkpoints (2 lines)
   601→	// The shadow tree captures the worktree state, which includes user edits made between checkpoints
   602→	shadowTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   603→		"main.go": "agent1\nagent2\nuser_between1\nuser_between2\nagent3\nagent4\nagent5\nagent6\nagent7\nagent8\nagent9\nagent10\n",
   604→	})
   605→
   606→	// Head (final commit): shadow + 1 more user line
   607→	headTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   608→		"main.go": "agent1\nagent2\nuser_between1\nuser_between2\nagent3\nagent4\nagent5\nagent6\nagent7\nagent8\nagent9\nagent10\nuser_after\n",
   609→	})
   610→
   611→	filesTouched := []string{"main.go"}
   612→
   613→	// PromptAttribution captured that 2 lines were added by user between checkpoints
   614→	// This helps separate user work from agent work, since shadow tree includes both
   615→	promptAttributions := []PromptAttribution{
   616→		{
   617→			CheckpointNumber: 2,
   618→			UserLinesAdded:   2, // user_between1, user_between2
   619→			UserLinesRemoved: 0,
   620→			UserAddedPerFile: map[string]int{"main.go": 2}, // User edited the agent-touched file
   621→		},
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	result := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   625→		baseTree, shadowTree, headTree, filesTouched, promptAttributions,
   626→	)
   627→
   628→	if result == nil {
   629→		t.Fatal("expected non-nil result")
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	// Expected calculation:
   633→	// - base → shadow: +12 lines added (includes agent + user between)
   634→	// - shadow → head: +1 line added (user after)
   635→	// - accumulatedUserAdded: 2 (from PromptAttributions)
   636→	// - totalAgentAdded: 12 - 2 = 10 (correctly separates user lines from agent work)
   637→	// - totalUserAdded: 2 + 1 = 3
   638→	// - agentLinesInCommit: 10
   639→	// - Total: 10 + 3 = 13
   640→	// - Agent percentage: 10/13 = 76.9%
   641→
   642→	if result.AgentLines != 10 {
   643→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, want 10 (excludes user lines in shadow snapshot)", result.AgentLines)
   644→	}
   645→	if result.HumanAdded != 3 {
   646→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 3 (2 between + 1 after)", result.HumanAdded)
   647→	}
   648→	if result.HumanModified != 0 {
   649→		t.Errorf("HumanModified = %d, want 0", result.HumanModified)
   650→	}
   651→	if result.HumanRemoved != 0 {
   652→		t.Errorf("HumanRemoved = %d, want 0", result.HumanRemoved)
   653→	}
   654→	if result.TotalCommitted != 13 {
   655→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, want 13 (10 + 3)", result.TotalCommitted)
   656→	}
   657→	if result.AgentPercentage < 76.8 || result.AgentPercentage > 77.0 {
   658→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want 76.9%%", result.AgentPercentage)
   659→	}
   660→}

   663→func TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_EmptyFilesTouched(t *testing.T) {

   676→
   677→// TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserEditsNonAgentFile tests the bug where
   678→// post-checkpoint user edits to files the agent never touched are undercounted.
   679→//
   680→// Bug scenario:
   681→//  1. Agent touches file1.go (added to filesTouched)
   682→//  2. User edits file2.go between checkpoints → captured in PromptAttributions
   683→//  3. User edits file2.go again AFTER last checkpoint, before commit
   684→//  4. BUG: Post-checkpoint calculation only looks at filesTouched (file1.go),
   685→//     missing the file2.go edits in step 3
   686→//
   687→// This causes undercounted user contributions and inflated agent percentage.
   688→func TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserEditsNonAgentFile(t *testing.T) {
   689→	// Base: two files
   690→	baseTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   691→		"file1.go": "package main\n",
   692→		"file2.go": "package util\n",
   693→	})
   694→
   695→	// Shadow (agent work): agent adds to file1.go only
   696→	// file2.go is NOT in shadow tree because it's not in filesTouched
   697→	shadowTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   698→		"file1.go": "package main\n\nfunc agent1() {}\nfunc agent2() {}\n",
   699→	})
   700→
   701→	// Head (final commit): user adds more to file2.go AFTER last checkpoint
   702→	// file2.go has: 1 base line + 2 accumulated + 2 post-checkpoint = 5 lines total
   703→	headTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   704→		"file1.go": "package main\n\nfunc agent1() {}\nfunc agent2() {}\n",
   705→		"file2.go": "package util\n\n// User edit 1\n// User edit 2\n// User edit 3\n",
   706→	})
   707→
   708→	// filesTouched only includes file1.go (agent-touched)
   709→	filesTouched := []string{"file1.go"}
   710→
   711→	// PromptAttributions captured user edits to file2.go between checkpoints
   712→	promptAttributions := []PromptAttribution{
   713→		{
   714→			CheckpointNumber: 1,
   715→			UserLinesAdded:   2, // User edit 1, 2 (between checkpoints)
   716→			UserLinesRemoved: 0,
   717→			UserAddedPerFile: map[string]int{"file2.go": 2}, // Tracks which file the edits were in
   718→		},
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	result := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   722→		baseTree, shadowTree, headTree, filesTouched, promptAttributions,
   723→	)
   724→
   725→	if result == nil {
   726→		t.Fatal("expected non-nil result")
   727→	}
   728→
   729→	// Expected calculation:
   730→	// - Agent added 3 lines to file1.go (2 functions + 1 blank)
   731→	// - User added 2 lines to file2.go between checkpoints (from PromptAttribution)
   732→	// - User added 2 MORE lines to file2.go after last checkpoint (post-checkpoint)
   733→	// - Total user added: 2 + 2 = 4
   734→	// - agentLinesInCommit: 3
   735→	// - Total: 3 + 4 = 7
   736→	// - Agent percentage: 3/7 = 42.9%
   737→	//
   738→	// BUG (if not fixed): Post-checkpoint calculation only looks at file1.go,
   739→	// so it would miss the 2 post-checkpoint edits to file2.go:
   740→	// - Total user added: 2 + 0 = 2 (WRONG)
   741→	// - Total: 3 + 2 = 5 (WRONG)
   742→	// - Agent percentage: 3/5 = 60% (WRONG, inflated)
   743→
   744→	t.Logf("Attribution: agent=%d, human_added=%d, total=%d, percentage=%.1f%%",
   745→		result.AgentLines, result.HumanAdded, result.TotalCommitted, result.AgentPercentage)
   746→
   747→	if result.AgentLines != 3 {
   748→		t.Errorf("AgentLines = %d, want 3", result.AgentLines)
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	if result.HumanAdded != 4 {
   752→		t.Errorf("HumanAdded = %d, want 4 (2 between + 2 post-checkpoint, including file agent never touched)",
   753→			result.HumanAdded)
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	if result.TotalCommitted != 7 {
   757→		t.Errorf("TotalCommitted = %d, want 7 (3 agent + 4 user)", result.TotalCommitted)
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	// Agent percentage should be 3/7 = 42.9%
   761→	if result.AgentPercentage < 42.8 || result.AgentPercentage > 43.0 {
   762→		t.Errorf("AgentPercentage = %.1f%%, want ~42.9%% (not inflated)", result.AgentPercentage)
   763→	}
   764→}
   765→
   766→// TestGetAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees tests the hash-based file change detection.
   767→func TestGetAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(t *testing.T) {
   768→	storer := memory.NewStorage()
   769→
   770→	// Helper to create a blob and return its hash
   771→	//nolint:errcheck // Test helper - errors would cause test failures anyway
   772→	createBlob := func(content string) plumbing.Hash {
   773→		blob := storer.NewEncodedObject()
   774→		blob.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
   775→		writer, _ := blob.Writer()
   776→		_, _ = writer.Write([]byte(content))
   777→		_ = writer.Close()
   778→		hash, _ := storer.SetEncodedObject(blob)
   779→		return hash
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	// Helper to create a tree from file entries
   783→	//nolint:errcheck // Test helper - errors would cause test failures anyway
   784→	createTree := func(files map[string]string) *object.Tree {
   785→		var entries []object.TreeEntry
   786→		for name, content := range files {
   787→			entries = append(entries, object.TreeEntry{
   788→				Name: name,
   789→				Mode: 0o100644,
   790→				Hash: createBlob(content),
   791→			})
   792→		}
   793→		// Sort entries by name (git requirement)
   794→		sort.Slice(entries, func(i, j int) bool {
   795→			return entries[i].Name < entries[j].Name
   796→		})
   797→
   798→		tree := &object.Tree{Entries: entries}
   799→		treeObj := storer.NewEncodedObject()
   800→		_ = tree.Encode(treeObj)
   801→		hash, _ := storer.SetEncodedObject(treeObj)
   802→
   803→		// Re-decode to get proper Tree object with hash
   804→		decodedTree, _ := object.GetTree(storer, hash)
   805→		return decodedTree
   806→	}
   807→
   808→	t.Run("both trees nil", func(t *testing.T) {
   809→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(nil, nil)
   810→		if result != nil {
   811→			t.Errorf("expected nil, got %v", result)
   812→		}
   813→	})
   814→
   815→	t.Run("tree1 nil (all files added)", func(t *testing.T) {
   816→		tree2 := createTree(map[string]string{
   817→			testFile1:  "content1",
   818→			"file2.go": "content2",
   819→		})
   820→
   821→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(nil, tree2)
   822→		sort.Strings(result)
   823→
   824→		if len(result) != 2 {
   825→			t.Fatalf("expected 2 changed files, got %d: %v", len(result), result)
   826→		}
   827→		if result[0] != testFile1 || result[1] != "file2.go" {
   828→			t.Errorf("expected [file1.go, file2.go], got %v", result)
   829→		}
   830→	})
   831→
   832→	t.Run("tree2 nil (all files deleted)", func(t *testing.T) {
   833→		tree1 := createTree(map[string]string{
   834→			testFile1: "content1",
   835→		})
   836→
   837→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(tree1, nil)
   838→
   839→		if len(result) != 1 || result[0] != testFile1 {
   840→			t.Errorf("expected [file1.go], got %v", result)
   841→		}
   842→	})
   843→
   844→	t.Run("identical trees (no changes)", func(t *testing.T) {
   845→		tree1 := createTree(map[string]string{
   846→			testFile1:  "same content",
   847→			"file2.go": "also same",
   848→		})
   849→		tree2 := createTree(map[string]string{
   850→			testFile1:  "same content",
   851→			"file2.go": "also same",
   852→		})
   853→
   854→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(tree1, tree2)
   855→
   856→		if len(result) != 0 {
   857→			t.Errorf("expected no changes, got %v", result)
   858→		}
   859→	})
   860→
   861→	t.Run("one file modified", func(t *testing.T) {
   862→		tree1 := createTree(map[string]string{
   863→			testFile1:      "original",
   864→			"unchanged.go": "stays same",
   865→		})
   866→		tree2 := createTree(map[string]string{
   867→			testFile1:      "modified",
   868→			"unchanged.go": "stays same",
   869→		})
   870→
   871→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(tree1, tree2)
   872→
   873→		if len(result) != 1 || result[0] != testFile1 {
   874→			t.Errorf("expected [file1.go], got %v", result)
   875→		}
   876→	})
   877→
   878→	t.Run("file added and deleted", func(t *testing.T) {
   879→		tree1 := createTree(map[string]string{
   880→			"deleted.go": "will be removed",
   881→			"stays.go":   "unchanged",
   882→		})
   883→		tree2 := createTree(map[string]string{
   884→			"added.go": "new file",
   885→			"stays.go": "unchanged",
   886→		})
   887→
   888→		result := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(tree1, tree2)
   889→		sort.Strings(result)
   890→
   891→		if len(result) != 2 {
   892→			t.Fatalf("expected 2 changed files, got %d: %v", len(result), result)
   893→		}
   894→		if result[0] != "added.go" || result[1] != "deleted.go" {
   895→			t.Errorf("expected [added.go, deleted.go], got %v", result)
   896→		}
   897→	})
   898→}
   899→
   900→// TestEstimateUserSelfModifications tests the LIFO heuristic for user self-modifications.
   901→func TestEstimateUserSelfModifications(t *testing.T) {
   902→	tests := []struct {
   903→		name                  string
   904→		accumulatedUserAdded  map[string]int
   905→		postCheckpointRemoved map[string]int
   906→		expectedSelfModified  int
   907→	}{
   908→		{
   909→			name:                  "no removals",
   910→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   911→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{},
   912→			expectedSelfModified:  0,
   913→		},
   914→		{
   915→			name:                  "removals less than user added",
   916→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   917→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"file.go": 3},
   918→			expectedSelfModified:  3, // All 3 removals are self-modifications
   919→		},
   920→		{
   921→			name:                  "removals equal to user added",
   922→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   923→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   924→			expectedSelfModified:  5, // All 5 removals are self-modifications
   925→		},
   926→		{
   927→			name:                  "removals exceed user added",
   928→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"file.go": 3},
   929→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   930→			expectedSelfModified:  3, // Only 3 are self-modifications, 2 must be agent lines
   931→		},
   932→		{
   933→			name:                  "no user additions to file",
   934→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{},
   935→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"file.go": 5},
   936→			expectedSelfModified:  0, // All removals target agent lines
   937→		},
   938→		{
   939→			name:                  "multiple files",
   940→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"a.go": 3, "b.go": 2},
   941→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"a.go": 2, "b.go": 4},
   942→			expectedSelfModified:  4, // 2 from a.go + 2 from b.go (capped at user additions)
   943→		},
   944→		{
   945→			name:                  "removal from file user never touched",
   946→			accumulatedUserAdded:  map[string]int{"a.go": 5},
   947→			postCheckpointRemoved: map[string]int{"b.go": 3},
   948→			expectedSelfModified:  0, // User never added to b.go, so all removals are agent lines
   949→		},
   950→	}
   951→
   952→	for _, tt := range tests {
   953→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   954→			result := estimateUserSelfModifications(tt.accumulatedUserAdded, tt.postCheckpointRemoved)
   955→			if result != tt.expectedSelfModified {
   956→				t.Errorf("estimateUserSelfModifications() = %d, want %d", result, tt.expectedSelfModified)
   957→			}
   958→		})
   959→	}
   960→}
   961→
   962→// TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserSelfModification tests the per-file tracking fix:
   963→// when a user modifies their own previously-added lines (not agent lines),
   964→// it should NOT reduce the agent's contribution.
   965→//
   966→// Bug scenario before fix:
   967→// 1. Agent adds 10 lines
   968→// 2. User adds 5 lines of their own (captured in PromptAttribution)
   969→// 3. User later removes 3 of their own lines and adds 3 different ones
   970→// 4. OLD: humanModified=3 was subtracted from agent lines (WRONG)
   971→// 5. NEW: humanModified=3 but userSelfModified=3, so agent lines unchanged (CORRECT)
   972→func TestCalculateAttributionWithAccumulated_UserSelfModification(t *testing.T) {
   973→	// Base: empty file
   974→	baseTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   975→		"main.go": "",
   976→	})
   977→
   978→	// Shadow (checkpoint state): agent added 10 lines, user added 5 lines between checkpoints
   979→	// The shadow includes both because it's a snapshot of the worktree at checkpoint time
   980→	shadowTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   981→		"main.go": "agent1\nagent2\nagent3\nagent4\nagent5\nagent6\nagent7\nagent8\nagent9\nagent10\nuser1\nuser2\nuser3\nuser4\nuser5\n",
   982→	})
   983→
   984→	// Head (commit state): user removed 3 of their own lines and added 3 different ones
   985→	// Agent lines are unchanged
   986→	headTree := buildTestTree(t, map[string]string{
   987→		"main.go": "agent1\nagent2\nagent3\nagent4\nagent5\nagent6\nagent7\nagent8\nagent9\nagent10\nuser1\nuser2\nnew_user1\nnew_user2\nnew_user3\n",
   988→	})
   989→
   990→	filesTouched := []string{"main.go"}
   991→
   992→	// PromptAttribution captured that user added 5 lines between checkpoints
   993→	promptAttributions := []PromptAttribution{
   994→		{
   995→			CheckpointNumber: 2,
   996→			UserLinesAdded:   5,
   997→			UserLinesRemoved: 0,
   998→			UserAddedPerFile: map[string]int{"main.go": 5}, // KEY: per-file tracking
   999→		},
  1000→	}
  1001→
  1002→	result := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
  1003→		baseTree, shadowTree, headTree, filesTouched, promptAttributions,
  1004→	)
  1005→
  1006→	if result == nil {
  1007→		t.Fatal("expected non-nil result")
  1008→	}
```

```
   165→func CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   166→	baseTree *object.Tree,

   170→	promptAttributions []PromptAttribution,
   171→) *checkpoint.InitialAttribution {
   172→	if len(filesTouched) == 0 {
   173→		return nil
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Sum accumulated user lines from prompt attributions
   177→	// Also aggregate per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking
   178→	var accumulatedUserAdded, accumulatedUserRemoved int
   179→	accumulatedUserAddedPerFile := make(map[string]int)
   180→	for _, pa := range promptAttributions {
   181→		accumulatedUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   182→		accumulatedUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   183→		// Merge per-file data from all prompt attributions
   184→		for filePath, added := range pa.UserAddedPerFile {
   185→			accumulatedUserAddedPerFile[filePath] += added
   186→		}
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	// Calculate attribution for agent-touched files
   190→	// IMPORTANT: shadowTree is a snapshot of the worktree at checkpoint time,
   191→	// which includes both agent work AND accumulated user edits (to agent-touched files).
   192→	// So base→shadow diff = (agent work + accumulated user work to these files).
   193→	var totalAgentAndUserWork int
   194→	var postCheckpointUserAdded, postCheckpointUserRemoved int
   195→	postCheckpointUserRemovedPerFile := make(map[string]int)
   196→
   197→	for _, filePath := range filesTouched {
   198→		baseContent := getFileContent(baseTree, filePath)
   199→		shadowContent := getFileContent(shadowTree, filePath)
   200→		headContent := getFileContent(headTree, filePath)
   201→
   202→		// Total work in shadow: base → shadow (agent + accumulated user work for this file)
   203→		_, workAdded, _ := diffLines(baseContent, shadowContent)
   204→		totalAgentAndUserWork += workAdded
   205→
   206→		// Post-checkpoint user edits: shadow → head (only post-checkpoint edits for this file)
   207→		_, postUserAdded, postUserRemoved := diffLines(shadowContent, headContent)
   208→		postCheckpointUserAdded += postUserAdded
   209→		postCheckpointUserRemoved += postUserRemoved
   210→
   211→		// Track per-file removals for self-modification estimation
   212→		if postUserRemoved > 0 {
   213→			postCheckpointUserRemovedPerFile[filePath] = postUserRemoved
   214→		}
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	// Calculate total user edits to non-agent files (files not in filesTouched)
   218→	// These files are not in the shadow tree, so base→head captures ALL their user edits
   219→	nonAgentFiles := getAllChangedFilesBetweenTrees(baseTree, headTree)
   220→	var allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles int
   221→	for _, filePath := range nonAgentFiles {
   222→		if slices.Contains(filesTouched, filePath) {
   223→			continue // Skip agent-touched files
   224→		}
   225→
   226→		baseContent := getFileContent(baseTree, filePath)
   227→		headContent := getFileContent(headTree, filePath)
   228→		_, userAdded, _ := diffLines(baseContent, headContent)
   229→		allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles += userAdded
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Separate accumulated edits by file type using per-file tracking data.
   233→	// This is precise because accumulatedUserAddedPerFile tells us exactly which files
   234→	// the user edited between checkpoints.
   235→	var accumulatedToAgentFiles, accumulatedToNonAgentFiles int
   236→	for filePath, added := range accumulatedUserAddedPerFile {
   237→		if slices.Contains(filesTouched, filePath) {
   238→			accumulatedToAgentFiles += added
   239→		} else {
   240→			accumulatedToNonAgentFiles += added
   241→		}
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	// Agent work = (base→shadow for agent files) - (accumulated user edits to agent files only)
   245→	totalAgentAdded := max(0, totalAgentAndUserWork-accumulatedToAgentFiles)
   246→
   247→	// Post-checkpoint edits to non-agent files = total edits - accumulated portion (never negative)
   248→	postToNonAgentFiles := max(0, allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles-accumulatedToNonAgentFiles)
   249→
   250→	// Total user contribution = accumulated (all files) + post-checkpoint (agent files) + post-checkpoint (non-agent files)
   251→	totalUserAdded := accumulatedUserAdded + postCheckpointUserAdded + postToNonAgentFiles
   252→	totalUserRemoved := accumulatedUserRemoved + postCheckpointUserRemoved
   253→
   254→	// Estimate modified lines (user changed existing lines)
   255→	// Lines that were both added and removed are treated as modifications.
   256→	totalHumanModified := min(totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved)
```

Now I understand the full code structure. The fix needs to be in the accumulated edits classification section. Instead of counting `accumulatedUserAdded` (which includes all files including uncommitted ones), I need to only count accumulated additions for files that are actually committed.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "\t// Separate accumulated edits by file type using per-file tracking data.\n\t// This is precise because accumulatedUserAddedPerFile tells us exactly which files\n\t// the user edited between checkpoints.\n\tvar accumulatedToAgentFiles, accumulatedToNonAgentFiles int\n\tfor filePath, added := range accumulatedUserAddedPerFile {\n\t\tif slices.Contains(filesTouched, filePath) {\n\t\t\taccumulatedToAgentFiles += added\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\taccumulatedToNonAgentFiles += added\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Agent work = (base→shadow for agent files) - (accumulated user edits to agent files only)\n\ttotalAgentAdded := max(0, totalAgentAndUserWork-accumulatedToAgentFiles)\n\n\t// Post-checkpoint edits to non-agent files = total edits - accumulated portion (never negative)\n\tpostToNonAgentFiles := max(0, allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles-accumulatedToNonAgentFiles)\n\n\t// Total user contribution = accumulated (all files) + post-checkpoint (agent files) + post-checkpoint (non-agent files)\n\ttotalUserAdded := accumulatedUserAdded + postCheckpointUserAdded + postToNonAgentFiles\n\ttotalUserRemoved := accumulatedUserRemoved + postCheckpointUserRemoved",
  "new_string": "\t// Separate accumulated edits by file type using per-file tracking data.\n\t// Only count changes to files that are actually committed:\n\t// - Agent-touched files (filesTouched)\n\t// - Non-agent files that appear in the commit (base→head diff)\n\t// Files not in either set are worktree-only changes (e.g., .claude/settings.json)\n\t// that should not affect attribution.\n\tcommittedNonAgentSet := make(map[string]struct{}, len(nonAgentFiles))\n\tfor _, f := range nonAgentFiles {\n\t\tif !slices.Contains(filesTouched, f) {\n\t\t\tcommittedNonAgentSet[f] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar accumulatedToAgentFiles, accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles int\n\tfor filePath, added := range accumulatedUserAddedPerFile {\n\t\tif slices.Contains(filesTouched, filePath) {\n\t\t\taccumulatedToAgentFiles += added\n\t\t} else if _, ok := committedNonAgentSet[filePath]; ok {\n\t\t\taccumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles += added\n\t\t}\n\t\t// else: file not committed (worktree-only), excluded from attribution\n\t}\n\n\t// Agent work = (base→shadow for agent files) - (accumulated user edits to agent files only)\n\ttotalAgentAdded := max(0, totalAgentAndUserWork-accumulatedToAgentFiles)\n\n\t// Post-checkpoint edits to non-agent files = total edits - accumulated portion (never negative)\n\tpostToNonAgentFiles := max(0, allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles-accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles)\n\n\t// Total user contribution = accumulated (committed files only) + post-checkpoint edits\n\trelevantAccumulatedUser := accumulatedToAgentFiles + accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles\n\ttotalUserAdded := relevantAccumulatedUser + postCheckpointUserAdded + postToNonAgentFiles\n\ttotalUserRemoved := accumulatedUserRemoved + postCheckpointUserRemoved"
}
```

```
   233→	// Only count changes to files that are actually committed:
   234→	// - Agent-touched files (filesTouched)
   235→	// - Non-agent files that appear in the commit (base→head diff)
   236→	// Files not in either set are worktree-only changes (e.g., .claude/settings.json)
   237→	// that should not affect attribution.
   238→	committedNonAgentSet := make(map[string]struct{}, len(nonAgentFiles))
   239→	for _, f := range nonAgentFiles {
   240→		if !slices.Contains(filesTouched, f) {
   241→			committedNonAgentSet[f] = struct{}{}
   242→		}
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	var accumulatedToAgentFiles, accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles int
   246→	for filePath, added := range accumulatedUserAddedPerFile {
   247→		if slices.Contains(filesTouched, filePath) {
   248→			accumulatedToAgentFiles += added
   249→		} else if _, ok := committedNonAgentSet[filePath]; ok {
   250→			accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles += added
   251→		}
   252→		// else: file not committed (worktree-only), excluded from attribution
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Agent work = (base→shadow for agent files) - (accumulated user edits to agent files only)
   256→	totalAgentAdded := max(0, totalAgentAndUserWork-accumulatedToAgentFiles)
   257→
   258→	// Post-checkpoint edits to non-agent files = total edits - accumulated portion (never negative)
   259→	postToNonAgentFiles := max(0, allUserEditsToNonAgentFiles-accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles)
   260→
   261→	// Total user contribution = accumulated (committed files only) + post-checkpoint edits
   262→	relevantAccumulatedUser := accumulatedToAgentFiles + accumulatedToCommittedNonAgentFiles
   263→	totalUserAdded := relevantAccumulatedUser + postCheckpointUserAdded + postToNonAgentFiles
   264→	// TODO: accumulatedUserRemoved also includes removals from uncommitted files,
   265→	// but we don't have per-file tracking for removals yet. In practice, removals
   266→	// from uncommitted files are rare and the impact is minor (could slightly reduce
   267→	// totalCommitted via pureUserRemoved). Add UserRemovedPerFile if this becomes an issue.
   268→	totalUserRemoved := accumulatedUserRemoved + postCheckpointUserRemoved
   269→
   270→	// Estimate modified lines (user changed existing lines)
   271→	// Lines that were both added and removed are treated as modifications.
```

> AGENT

Here's a summary of the bug and fix:

## Root Cause

`calculatePromptAttributionAtStart()` captures ALL worktree changes at prompt start, including files that may never be committed (like `.claude/settings.json`). It filters `.entire/` paths but not other untracked directories. When `CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated()` later computed the final attribution, it included the full `accumulatedUserAdded` (from all files, including uncommitted ones) in `totalUserAdded`, inflating the denominator and deflating the agent percentage.

In the E2E test:
- `.claude/settings.json` had 84 lines in the worktree (from Claude Code setup)
- `docs/example.md` had 17 lines (agent-created)
- Only `docs/example.md` was committed
- But `totalUserAdded=84` was counted, giving `totalCommitted=101` and `agentPercentage=16.8%`

## Fix

In `CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated()`, I changed the accumulated edits classification to only count changes to files that are actually committed:
1. **Agent-touched files** (`filesTouched`)
2. **Committed non-agent files** (files in the base→head diff that aren't in `filesTouched`)

Files not in either set (worktree-only changes like `.claude/settings.json`) are excluded from the attribution calculation. This is the correct behavior since attribution should only measure the composition of what was actually committed.

> AGENT

Yes, this is the exact same bug. The user's repo had:
- Parent commit: `README.md` (1 line)
- Agent commit: `entire_haiku.txt` (3 lines)
- But attribution shows: `human_added: 107,873,206` (107M lines!)

The 107M lines came from worktree files captured by `calculatePromptAttributionAtStart` at prompt time — likely large `.claude/` transcript files or other untracked content in `/Users/kevin/Dev`. None of those 107M lines were actually committed, but the old code counted them all in `totalUserAdded`.

Our fix in `CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated` directly addresses this: accumulated user additions are now only counted for files that appear in either `filesTouched` (agent-touched) or the base→head diff (committed non-agent files). The 107M lines from uncommitted worktree files would be completely excluded, giving the correct result of `agent_percentage ≈ 75%` (3 agent lines out of 4 total committed: 3 haiku + 1 README).

The E2E test `TestLineAttributionReasonable` also has a `SkipKnownIssue(t, "ENT-344", ...)` referencing this exact issue — with our fix, that skip can be removed to let the test run.

```
    23→			t.Fatalf("agent failed: %v", err)
    24→		}
    25→		testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "docs/example.md")
    26→
    27→		s.Git(t, "add", "docs/")
    28→		s.Git(t, "commit", "-m", "Add example.md")
    29→
    30→		testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 15*time.Second)
    31→
    32→		cpID := testutil.AssertHasCheckpointTrailer(t, s.Dir, "HEAD")
    33→		sm := testutil.ReadSessionMetadata(t, s.Dir, cpID, 0)
    34→
    35→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.AgentLines, 0,
    36→			"agent lines should be > 0")
    37→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted, 0,
    38→			"total committed should be > 0")
    39→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.AgentPercentage, 50.0,
    40→			"agent created 100%% of content, percentage should be > 50%%")
    41→	})
    42→}
    43→
    44→// TestAttributionOnAgentCommit: agent creates a file and commits it in the
    45→// same prompt (interactive session). The first commit's checkpoint should have
    46→// initial_attribution populated.
    47→func TestAttributionOnAgentCommit(t *testing.T) {
    48→	testutil.SkipKnownIssue(t, "GH #344", "initial_attribution missing on first agent-initiated commit")
    49→
    50→	testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 3*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
    51→		prompt := s.Agent.PromptPattern()
    52→
    53→		session, err := s.Agent.StartSession(ctx, s.Dir)
    54→		if err != nil {
    55→			t.Fatalf("start session: %v", err)
    56→		}
    57→		if session == nil {
    58→			t.Skipf("agent %s does not support interactive mode", s.Agent.Name())
    59→		}
    60→		defer func() { _ = session.Close() }()
    61→
    62→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 30*time.Second); err != nil {
    63→			t.Fatalf("waiting for initial prompt: %v", err)
    64→		}
    65→
    66→		s.Send(t, session, "create a file called hello.txt containing 'hello world', then commit it. Do not ask for confirmation.")
    67→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 60*time.Second); err != nil {
    68→			t.Fatalf("waiting for prompt after agent commit: %v", err)
    69→		}
    70→		testutil.AssertNewCommits(t, s, 1)
    71→
    72→		testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 15*time.Second)
    73→		cpID := testutil.AssertHasCheckpointTrailer(t, s.Dir, "HEAD")
    74→		sm := testutil.ReadSessionMetadata(t, s.Dir, cpID, 0)
    75→
    76→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.AgentLines, 0,
    77→			"agent lines should be > 0 on first agent commit")
    78→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted, 0,
    79→			"total committed should be > 0 on first agent commit")
    80→	})
    81→}
    82→
    83→// TestAttributionMultiCommitSameSession: two prompts in the same interactive
    84→// session, agent modifies the same file and commits both times. The second
    85→// checkpoint's initial_attribution should have non-zero values.
    86→func TestAttributionMultiCommitSameSession(t *testing.T) {
    87→	testutil.SkipKnownIssue(t, "GH #344", "second commit in same session has zeroed initial_attribution")
    88→
    89→	testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 4*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
    90→		prompt := s.Agent.PromptPattern()

   100→
   101→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 30*time.Second); err != nil {

   103→		}
   104→
   105→		// First prompt: create file and commit.
   106→		s.Send(t, session, "create a file called poem.txt with a short poem about coding, then commit it. Do not ask for confirmation.")
   107→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 60*time.Second); err != nil {
   108→			t.Fatalf("waiting for prompt after first commit: %v", err)

   112→		testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 15*time.Second)

   114→
   115→		// Second prompt: modify same file and commit again.
   116→		s.Send(t, session, "add another stanza to poem.txt about debugging, then commit it. Do not ask for confirmation.")
   117→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 60*time.Second); err != nil {

   120→		testutil.AssertNewCommits(t, s, 2)
   121→
   122→		testutil.WaitForCheckpointAdvanceFrom(t, s.Dir, cpBranch1, 15*time.Second)
   123→		cpID2 := testutil.AssertHasCheckpointTrailer(t, s.Dir, "HEAD")
   124→		sm := testutil.ReadSessionMetadata(t, s.Dir, cpID2, 0)
   125→

   127→			"agent lines should be > 0 on second commit")
   128→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.TotalCommitted, 0,
   129→			"total committed should be > 0 on second commit")
   130→		assert.Greater(t, sm.InitialAttribution.AgentPercentage, 50.0,
   131→			"agent wrote all content, percentage should be > 50%%")
   132→	})
   133→}

   135→// TestShadowBranchCleanedAfterAgentCommit: after an agent creates a file
   136→// and commits it, there should be no lingering shadow branches. Shadow
   137→// branches match entire/* but are not entire/checkpoints/*.
   138→func TestShadowBranchCleanedAfterAgentCommit(t *testing.T) {
   139→	testutil.SkipKnownIssue(t, "GH #344", "shadow branch persists after agent commits all changes")
   140→
   141→	testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 3*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
   142→		prompt := s.Agent.PromptPattern()
   143→
   144→		session, err := s.Agent.StartSession(ctx, s.Dir)
   145→		if err != nil {
   146→			t.Fatalf("start session: %v", err)
   147→		}
   148→		if session == nil {
   149→			t.Skipf("agent %s does not support interactive mode", s.Agent.Name())
   150→		}
   151→		defer func() { _ = session.Close() }()
   152→
   153→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 30*time.Second); err != nil {
   154→			t.Fatalf("waiting for initial prompt: %v", err)
   155→		}
   156→
   157→		s.Send(t, session, "create a file called hello.txt containing 'hello world', then commit it. Do not ask for confirmation.")
   158→		if _, err = session.WaitFor(prompt, 60*time.Second); err != nil {
   159→			t.Fatalf("waiting for prompt after agent commit: %v", err)
   160→		}
   161→		testutil.AssertNewCommits(t, s, 1)
   162→
   163→		testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 15*time.Second)
   164→
   165→		// List all entire/* branches, excluding entire/checkpoints/*.
   166→		// Shadow branches (entire/{hash}-{hash}) share the entire/ prefix
   167→		// with the checkpoint branch (entire/checkpoints/v1).
   168→		branches := testutil.GitOutput(t, s.Dir, "for-each-ref", "--format=%(refname:short)", "refs/heads/entire/")
   169→		var shadow []string
   170→		for _, b := range strings.Split(branches, "\n") {
   171→			b = strings.TrimSpace(b)
   172→			if b == "" || strings.HasPrefix(b, "entire/checkpoints") {
   173→				continue
   174→			}
   175→			shadow = append(shadow, b)
   176→		}
   177→		assert.Empty(t, shadow,
   178→			"shadow branches should be cleaned up after agent commit, found: %v", shadow)
   179→	})
   180→}
```

> AGENT

The issue is clear from the logs: when the agent commits mid-turn, **no shadow branch exists** (no `SaveChanges` was called yet). The condensation runs with `checkpoints_condensed: 0` and no shadow tree, so attribution can't be calculated. This is a different bug from what we fixed — it's about the mid-turn agent commit path missing attribution.

Let me check `calculateSessionAttributions` to see how it handles the missing shadow tree.

```
    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}

   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   108→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   109→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   110→//
   111→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveChanges called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   112→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   113→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   114→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   115→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   116→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   117→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   118→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   119→
   120→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   121→	if hasShadowBranch {
   122→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   123→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   124→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   125→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   126→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveChanges may have been skipped if the
   127→		// last turn had no code changes).
   128→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   129→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   130→		if err != nil {
   131→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   132→		}
   133→	} else {
   134→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveChanges.
   135→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   136→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   137→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   138→		}
   139→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   140→		if err != nil {
   141→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   142→		}
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   146→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   147→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   148→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   149→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   150→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   151→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   152→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   153→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   154→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   155→				}
   156→			}
   157→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   158→		}
   159→
   160→		// If extraction failed or returned empty, use committedFiles as fallback.
   161→		// This handles mid-session commits where transcript parsing may not find files
   162→		// but we know what was committed.
   163→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   164→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   165→			for f := range committedFiles {
   166→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   167→			}
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Get checkpoint store
   172→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Get author info
   178→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   179→	// Attribution calculation requires shadow branch reference; skip if mid-session commit
   180→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   181→	if hasShadowBranch {
   182→		attribution = calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   183→	}
   184→	// Get current branch name
   185→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   186→
   187→	// Generate summary if enabled
   188→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   189→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   190→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   191→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   192→
   193→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   194→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   195→		// For Gemini (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   196→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   197→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   198→			scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   199→		} else {
   200→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   201→		}
   202→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   203→			var err error
   204→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   205→			if err != nil {
   206→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   207→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   208→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   209→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   210→			} else {
   211→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   212→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   213→			}
   214→		}
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   218→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   219→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   220→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   221→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   222→		Branch:                      branchName,
   223→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   224→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   225→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   226→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   227→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   228→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   229→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   230→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   231→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   232→		TurnID:                      state.TurnID,
   233→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   234→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   235→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   236→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   237→		Summary:                     summary,
   238→		SessionTranscriptPath:       homeRelativePath(state.TranscriptPath),
   239→	}); err != nil {
   240→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	return &CondenseResult{
   244→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   245→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   246→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   247→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   248→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   249→	}, nil
   250→}
   251→
   252→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   253→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   254→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   255→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   256→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   257→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   258→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   259→	if headErr != nil {
   260→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   261→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   262→	} else {
   263→		headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   264→		if commitErr != nil {
   265→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   266→				slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   267→		} else {
   268→			headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   269→			if treeErr != nil {
   270→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   271→					slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   272→			} else {
   273→				// Get shadow branch tree (checkpoint tree - what the agent wrote)
   274→				shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   275→				if shadowErr != nil {
   276→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   277→						slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   278→						slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   279→				} else {
   280→					shadowTree, shadowTreeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   281→					if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   282→						logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   283→							slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   284→					} else {
   285→						// Get base tree (state before session started)
   286→						var baseTree *object.Tree
   287→						attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   288→						if attrBase == "" {
   289→							attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   290→						}
   291→						if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   292→							if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   293→								baseTree = tree
   294→							} else {
   295→								logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   296→									slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   297→							}
   298→						} else {
   299→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   300→								slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   301→								slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   302→						}
   303→
   304→						// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   305→						var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   306→						for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   307→							totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   308→							totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   309→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   310→								slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   311→								slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   312→								slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   313→								slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   314→								slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   315→								slog.Int("index", i))
   316→						}
   317→
   318→						attribution = CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   319→							baseTree,
   320→							shadowTree,
   321→							headTree,
   322→							sessionData.FilesTouched,
   323→							state.PromptAttributions,
   324→						)
   325→
   326→						if attribution != nil {
   327→							logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   328→								slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   329→								slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   330→								slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   331→								slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   332→								slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   333→								slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   334→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   335→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   336→								slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   337→						}
   338→					}
   339→				}
   340→			}
   341→		}
   342→	}
   343→	return attribution
   344→}
   345→
   346→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   347→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   348→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   349→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   350→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   351→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   352→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   353→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   354→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   355→	if err != nil {
   356→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   360→	if err != nil {
   361→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   365→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   366→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   367→
   368→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   369→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   370→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   371→	var fullTranscript string
   372→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   373→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   374→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   375→		}
   376→	}
   377→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   378→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   379→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   380→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   381→				fullTranscript = content
   382→			}
   383→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   384→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   385→				fullTranscript = content
   386→			}
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   391→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   392→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   393→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   394→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   395→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   399→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   400→
   401→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   402→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   403→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	return data, nil
   407→}
   408→
   409→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   410→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   411→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   412→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   413→
   414→	// Read the live transcript
   415→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   416→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   420→	if err != nil {
   421→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   425→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   429→	data.Transcript = liveData
   430→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   431→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   432→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   433→
   434→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   435→	// (no SaveChanges/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   436→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   437→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   438→	} else {
   439→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   440→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   444→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   445→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	return data, nil
   449→}
   450→
   451→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   452→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   453→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   454→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   455→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   456→	if content == "" {
   457→		return 0
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   461→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   462→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   463→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   464→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   465→		}
   466→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   467→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   468→			return 0
   469→		}
   470→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   474→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   475→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   476→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   477→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   478→	}
   479→	return len(allLines)
   480→}
   481→
   482→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   483→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   484→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   485→	if content == "" {
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   490→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   491→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   492→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   493→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   494→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   495→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   496→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   497→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   498→				}
   499→			}
   500→			return cleaned
   501→		}
   502→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   503→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   504→			return nil
   505→		}
   506→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   510→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   511→}
   512→
   513→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   514→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   515→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   516→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   517→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   518→	if len(data) == 0 {
   519→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   520→	}
   521→
   522→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   523→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   524→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   525→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   526→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   527→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   528→		}
   529→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   530→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   531→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   532→		}
   533→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   537→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   538→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   539→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   540→	}
   541→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   542→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   543→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   544→	}
   545→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   546→}
   547→
   548→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   549→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   550→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   551→	var prompts []string
   552→	for _, line := range lines {
   553→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   554→		if line == "" {
   555→			continue
   556→		}
   557→
   558→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   559→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   560→			continue
   561→		}
   562→
   563→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   564→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   565→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   566→			continue
   567→		}
   568→
   569→		// Extract message content
   570→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   571→		if !ok {
   572→			continue
   573→		}
   574→
   575→		// Handle string content
   576→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   577→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   578→			if cleaned != "" {
   579→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   580→			}
   581→			continue
   582→		}
   583→
   584→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   585→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   586→			var texts []string
   587→			for _, item := range arr {
   588→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   589→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   590→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   591→							texts = append(texts, text)
   592→						}
   593→					}
   594→				}
   595→			}
   596→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   597→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   598→				if cleaned != "" {
   599→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   600→				}
   601→			}
   602→		}
   603→	}
   604→	return prompts
   605→}
   606→
   607→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   608→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   609→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   610→		return nil
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	var buf strings.Builder
   614→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   615→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   616→
   617→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   618→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   619→		displayPrompt := prompt
   620→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   621→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   622→		}
   623→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   624→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   625→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	return []byte(buf.String())
   629→}
   630→
   631→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   632→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   633→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   634→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   635→
   636→	// Load session state
   637→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   638→	if err != nil {
   639→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   640→	}
   641→	if state == nil {
   642→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Open repository
   646→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   647→	if err != nil {
   648→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   649→	}
   650→
   651→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   652→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   653→	if err != nil {
   654→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   655→	}
   656→
   657→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   658→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   659→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   660→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   661→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   662→
   663→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   664→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   665→		// Just clean up the state file.
   666→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   667→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   668→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   669→		)
   670→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   671→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   672→		}
   673→		return nil
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Condense the session
   677→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, nil)
   678→	if err != nil {
   679→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   683→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   684→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   685→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   686→	)
   687→
   688→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   689→	state.StepCount = 0
   690→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   691→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   692→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   693→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   694→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   695→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   696→
   697→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   698→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   702→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   703→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   704→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   705→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   706→		)
   707→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	return nil
   711→}
   712→
   713→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   714→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   715→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   716→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   717→	if err != nil {
   718→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	for _, state := range allStates {
   722→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   723→			continue
   724→		}
   725→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   726→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   727→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   728→			return nil
   729→		}
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   733→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   734→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   735→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   736→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   737→			return nil
   738→		}
   739→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   740→	}
   741→	return nil
   742→}
   743→
```

The issue is clear from `manual_commit_condensation.go:179-183`:

```go
// Attribution calculation requires shadow branch reference; skip if mid-session commit
var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
if hasShadowBranch {
    attribution = calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
}
```

When the agent commits mid-turn (before `SaveChanges`/`Stop`), no shadow branch exists, so **attribution is completely skipped** — it stays `nil`.

This is a **separate bug** from what we fixed. Our fix addressed inflated attribution from uncommitted worktree files. These two tests (`TestAttributionOnAgentCommit`, `TestAttributionMultiCommitSameSession`) expose a second issue: **no attribution at all for agent-initiated commits**.

The fix is straightforward: when there's no shadow branch, use the HEAD tree as the shadow tree. Since the agent just committed, `HEAD` contains the agent's work, and `shadow→head` diff would correctly be 0 (no user edits after the agent committed).
